Method: 提出三个关键创新：(1) 时空蛇演化策略(SSES)，利用状态空间模型捕获蛇轮廓上双向空间依赖和跨演化步骤的时间动态；(2) 轮廓形态感知Mamba(CMAM)，量化局部轮廓形态以调制Mamba2 SSD双形式中的结构化注意力掩码，增强对输入序列元素的相对重要性感知；(3) 文本提示协同双头蛇(TCDHS)，融合文本提示线索并将演化后的轮廓信息传递到基础检测头，改善deep snake工作流并减少错误检测。

Result: 在覆盖不同器官和成像模态的五个数据集上进行综合评估，TEAMS在mDice和mBF指标上均优于现有的语义分割和deep snake分割方法，例如在脊柱数据集上相对提升6.9%的mDice和9.1%的mBF。

Conclusion: TEAMS展现了作为多种医学图像分割场景下可靠工具的潜力，通过视觉-语言Mamba框架有效融合了时空演化、形态感知和文本提示等多种机制，显著提升了蛇形实例分割的性能。

Abstract: Deep snake is a promising family of instance segmentation methods that accurately predicts object-level contours, thereby overcoming common pixel-level misclassification issues such as mask cavities and jagged edges in semantic segmentation approaches. However, existing deep snake methods face challenges in handling complex morphological variations, accurately capturing fine-grained organ details, and correcting base detection errors. To mitigate these limitations, we propose a cohesive Text-prompted spatiotEmporal dual-heAd Mamba Snake (TEAMS), a novel vision-language Mamba snake framework with three key innovations: (1) A Spatiotemporal Snake Evolution Strategy (SSES) is introduced to tackle complex morphological variations by capturing bidirectional spatial dependencies along the snake contour and temporal dynamics across evolution steps in a state space model. (2) A Contour Morphology-Aware Mamba (CMAM) is proposed to quantify local contour morphologies to modulate the structured attention mask in the Mamba2 SSD dual form, which extends Mamba's capability to perceive the relative importance of its input sequence elements for better delineation of fine-grained organ details. (3) A Text-prompted Collaborative Dual-Head Snake (TCDHS) is designed to incorporate cues from textual prompts and transfer the evolved contour information to the base detection head, which enhances the deep snake workflow and mitigates wrong detections. Comprehensive evaluations on five datasets covering different organs and imaging modalities demonstrate that TEAMS outperforms existing semantic and deep snake segmentation methods (e.g., relative mDice/mBF improvements of 6.9%/9.1% in a spinal dataset), underscoring its potential as a reliable tool across diverse medical image segmentation scenarios.

</details>


### [34] [TF-CADE: Foreground-Concentrated Text-Video Alignment for Zero-Shot Temporal Action Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17422)
*Yearang Lee,Ho-Joong Kim,Seong-Whan Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TF-CADE方法，通过文本-前景集中对齐解决零样本时序动作检测中动作类别语义区分不足的问题，在分布内和跨数据集泛化设置下均取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本时序动作检测方法虽通过推进文本-视频对齐架构取得一定成效，但在捕捉动作类别间的语义区分方面仍然不足，导致产生与文本无关的预测结果。

Method: 提出TF-CADE框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) Action Concentrate Aggregation (ACA)，提取动作集中分数并将时序上有信息量的视频片段聚合为前景加权视频嵌入，增强文本与视频特征的语义一致性并提升类间可分性；(2) Certainty-based Confidence Re-weighting (CCR)策略，利用前景感知相似度精细化每个片段的置信度分数，在推理时有效抑制不相关动作类别。

Result: TF-CADE在分布内（in-distribution）设置下达到最先进性能，同时在跨数据集泛化到未见动作类别的场景中表现优异。

Conclusion: 通过显式对齐文本信息与动作相关的前景区域，TF-CADE有效解决了零样本时序动作检测中语义区分不足的问题，同时具备良好的跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Zero-Shot Temporal Action Detection (ZSTAD) aims to lo- calize and recognize action instances from unseen action categories in untrimmed videos. Although existing meth- ods have shown effectiveness by advancing architectural text-video alignment, they still struggle with capturing se- mantic distinctions between action classes, resulting in text- irrelevant predictions. To address this issue, we propose a Text-Foreground Concentrated Alignment for zero-shot temporal action DEtector (TF-CADE) that explicitly aligns textual information with action-relevant foreground regions. Specifically, we introduce Action Concentrate Aggregation (ACA), which extracts action concentrate scores to aggregate temporally informative video segments into a foreground- weighted video embedding. This foreground concentrated alignment enhances the semantic consistency between text and video features and improves inter-class discriminabil- ity. In addition, a Certainty-based Confidence Re-weighting (CCR) strategy refines per-snippet confidence scores by lever- aging foreground-aware similarity, effectively suppressing irrelevant action classes during inference. Extensive evalua- tions show that our TF-CADE not only achieves state-of-the- art performance under in-distribution settings but also excels in cross-dataset generalization to unseen action classes.

</details>


### [35] [GSToken: Geometry-Structured Gaussian Tokens for Compact 3D Medical Image Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17425)
*Xiaoduo Li,Quan Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次将高斯token引入多模态脑肿瘤分割任务，每个token不仅携带语义特征，还携带学习到的3D中心、各向异性尺度和方向，赋予表征显式的几何支持。同时提出冻结token效用评估协议，通过固定容量序列化契约和共享轻量Transformer探针，在严格匹配条件下比较不同tokenizer的信息保留能力。多种子配对统计检验表明GSToken在冻结探测下显著优于容量匹配的自适应基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（固定patch编码或TokenLearner等学习注意力池化）在压缩3D体积为token序列时会丢弃显式空间形状信息，使token无法表达病灶形态或空间范围；且端到端评估将tokenizer的信息保留与下游解码器重建能力纠缠在一起，缺乏统一容量契约，难以归因性能差异。

Method: 1) 提出GSToken：每个token包含语义特征+学习的3D中心、各向异性尺度与方向，以极低参数代价赋予表征显式几何支撑；2) 提出冻结token效用评估协议：冻结训练好的tokenizer，将其输出转换为固定容量序列化契约，用共享轻量Transformer探针在严格匹配条件下独立测量各tokenizer的信息保留量；3) 采用多种子配对统计检验进行验证。

Result: GSToken在冻结探测下一致且显著优于容量匹配的自适应基线，在所有肿瘤亚区域、表面及距离指标上均具有统一优势。

Conclusion: 在token中显式编码空间几何可显著提升体积表征的信息密度，为紧凑3D医学图像表征及下游读取提供了新的设计原则。

Abstract: Effective segmentation of multi-modal MRI is central to improving neural network accuracy in brain tumor recognition. Existing methods typically compress 3D volumes into token sequences via fixed patch encoding or learned attention pooling (e.g., TokenLearner). However, these compression schemes discard explicit spatial shape information; the resulting tokens convey no notion of lesion morphology or spatial extent. Meanwhile, end-to-end evaluation entangles a tokenizer's information retention with the reconstruction capacity of the downstream decoder, and the lack of a unified capacity contract across methods makes performance differences difficult to attribute. In this paper, we introduce Gaussian tokens to multi-modal brain tumor segmentation for the first time: each token carries not only a semantic feature but also a learned 3D center, anisotropic scale, and orientation, endowing the representation with explicit geometric support at negligible parameter cost. We further propose a frozen-token utility evaluation protocol: the trained tokenizer is frozen, its output is cast into a fixed-capacity serialized contract, and a shared lightweight Transformer probe independently measures each tokenizer's retained information under strictly matched conditions. Multi-seed paired statistical testing shows that GSToken consistently and substantially outperforms capacity-matched adaptive baselines under frozen probing, with uniform advantages across all tumor sub-regions, surface, and distance metrics. These results demonstrate that explicitly encoding spatial geometry within tokens significantly improves the information density of volumetric representations, offering a new design principle for compact 3D medical image representation and downstream reading.

</details>


### [36] [SemComp-Bench: Benchmarking Semantic Task Completion in Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17426)
*Keyu Tu,Zhuowei Chen,Mengqi Huang,Yuxin Wang,Jiahao Zhu,Zhendong Mao,Yongdong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出语义任务完成视频生成（Semantic Task Completion Video Generation），一个以结果为导向的视频生成任务，要求同时实现预期结果和语义对齐，并构建了SemComp-Data数据集与SemComp-Bench评估协议来系统评测该任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成任务主要关注外观一致性和中间步骤的完整呈现，而缺乏对任务结果达成与否的语义级评估。本文旨在提出一个面向结果导向的视频生成任务，以弥补这一研究空白。

Method: 提出SemComp-Data数据集（覆盖6个领域，通过四阶段可扩展的curation流程将原始视频标准化为标准实例，每个实例包含参考图像、详细指令、简要指令和以结果为中心的视频片段），并提出SemComp-Bench评估协议，使用视觉语言模型回答结构化二元问题，分别报告OA分数（结果达成）和GR分数（生成可靠性）。

Result: 对代表性视频生成模型的实验表明，在实现预期结果的同时维持参考图像中任务相关语义对齐仍然具有挑战性。

Conclusion: 语义任务完成视频生成是一个具有挑战性的任务，现有视频生成模型在结果达成与语义对齐方面仍有不足，本文提供的数据集和评估协议可支持该方向的系统性研究。

Abstract: We introduce Semantic Task Completion Video Generation, an outcome-oriented video generation task. Under this formulation, success requires both achievement of the intended outcome and semantic grounding. Semantic grounding characterizes the correspondence between the reference image and the generated outcome in terms of high-level semantics relevant to the task. Evaluation focuses on the generated outcome and requires neither the presentation of a complete sequence of intermediate task steps nor conventional appearance consistency with the reference image. To support systematic evaluation, we construct SemComp-Data, an evaluation dataset covering six domains. Each instance comprises a reference image, a detailed instruction, a brief instruction, and an outcome-centric video clip. A scalable four-stage curation pipeline converts raw videos into standardized SemComp-Data instances. We further introduce SemComp-Bench, an evaluation protocol that uses a vision-language model (VLM) to answer structured binary questions. SemComp-Bench reports the OA Score and the GR Score for Outcome Achievement and Generation Reliability, respectively. Experiments on representative video generation models show that achieving intended outcomes while maintaining task-relevant semantic grounding in reference images remains challenging.

</details>


### [37] [Counterfactual Anatomy-guided Spatial-Temporal Decoding for Annotation-Free Hallucination Mitigation in Medical VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17427)
*Yifan Lu,Adinath Dukre,Abhijit Das,Ziyun Zou,Haolin Yang,Yutong Xie,Imran Razzak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CAST（Counterfactual Anatomy-guided Spatial-Temporal decoding）框架，一种完全在推理阶段运行、无需人工标注的医学视觉语言模型幻觉缓解方法。CAST通过自动解剖区域发现和反事实干预选择关键区域，并结合分类器无关引导与逐步时间对比的统一对比解码过程，在SLAKE和MIMIC-CXR数据集上一致优于强基线和依赖真实标注的解码策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉语言模型（Med-VLMs）在医学视觉问答中表现出色，但仍容易出现幻觉，生成缺乏图像证据支持的临床表述。现有的解码阶段缓解方法要么缺乏解剖学感知，要么严重依赖真实标注，限制了其实际适用性。

Method: CAST框架包含三个关键步骤：（1）通过广泛的医学分割自动发现与查询相关的解剖区域；（2）基于遮挡下答案似然度下降的反事实干预，选择紧凑且具有因果信息的关键区域；（3）由选定区域引导，执行统一的对比解码过程，结合分类器无关引导（classifier-free guidance）以校正空间注意力，以及逐步时间对比（stepwise temporal contrast）以调节生成动态。

Result: 在SLAKE和MIMIC-CXR两个数据集上、针对三个Med-VLMs的实验中，CAST一致优于强基线方法，并超过了依赖真实标注的解码策略。

Conclusion: 紧凑且自动选择的区域无需专家标注即可提供高效的对比引导，为改善空间定位和减少幻觉提供了实用且可泛化的解决方案。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (Med-VLMs) have demonstrated strong performance on medical visual question answering, yet they remain prone to hallucination, generating clinically unsupported statements that are insufficiently grounded in image evidence. Mitigation methods applied during decoding offer a practical solution, but they typically lack anatomical awareness or rely heavily on ground truth annotations, which limits their applicability. We propose Counterfactual Anatomy-guided Spatial-Temporal decoding (CAST), a framework that operates entirely during inference and requires no manual annotations for anatomically grounded hallucination mitigation. CAST automatically discovers anatomical regions relevant to the given query through broad medical segmentation. It then selects a compact, causally informative area using counterfactual intervention based on the drop in answer likelihood under occlusion. Guided by this chosen region, CAST performs a unified contrastive decoding process, combining classifier-free guidance to correct spatial attention with stepwise temporal contrast to regulate generation dynamics. Experiments on the SLAKE and MIMIC-CXR datasets across three Med-VLMs demonstrate that CAST consistently outperforms strong baselines and surpasses decoding strategies reliant on ground truth. Our results indicate that compact, automatically selected regions provide highly effective contrastive guidance without expert annotations, offering a practical and generalizable solution for improving spatial grounding and reducing hallucinations. Code is available at https://github.com/csyifan/CAST.

</details>


### [38] [NGS-Marker: Robust Native Watermarking for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17447)
*Hao Qin,Yukai Sun,Luyuan Chen,Mengxu Lu,Feng Zhang,Ming Kong,Zhenhong Du,Qiang Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NGS-Marker是一个面向3D高斯泼溅(3DGS)的原生水印框架，通过联合训练的水印注入器和消息解码器，以及基于梯度的渐进式注入策略，实现对场景全覆盖的鲁棒所有权解码，有效防御局部侵权攻击，并支持混合保护和多模态水印。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS水印技术主要关注通过预训练解码器保护渲染图像，而忽略了底层3D高斯原语的安全，尤其是在对抗"局部侵权"(Partial Infringement)攻击（攻击者提取并重用部分高斯子集）时效果不佳。

Method: 提出NGS-Marker框架，包含：(1) 联合训练的注入器和解码器；(2) 基于梯度的渐进式水印注入策略确保全场景覆盖；(3) 与间接水印相结合的混合保护方案；(4) 支持多模态水印。

Result: 大量实验表明NGS-Marker能有效防御局部侵权攻击，同时为实际部署提供灵活的实用性。

Conclusion: NGS-Marker为3DGS提供了一种原生的、能抵御局部侵权的版权保护方案，填补了现有方法在3D高斯原语层面保护的空白，并具备良好的扩展性和部署灵活性。

Abstract: With the rapid development and adoption of 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), the need for effective copyright protection has become increasingly critical. Existing watermarking techniques for 3DGS mainly focus on protecting rendered images via pre-trained decoders, leaving the underlying 3D Gaussian primitives vulnerable to misuse. In particular, they are ineffective against Partial Infringement, where an adversary extracts and reuses only a subset of Gaussians. In this paper, we propose NGS-Marker, a novel native watermarking framework for 3DGS. It integrates a jointly trained watermark injector and message decoder, and employs a gradientbased progressive injection strategy to ensure full-scene coverage. This enables robust ownership decoding from any local region. We further extend NGS-Marker with hybrid protection (combining native and indirect watermarks) and support for multimodal watermarking. Extensive experiments demonstrate that NGS-Marker effectively defends against partial infringement while offering practical flexibility for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [39] [S$^3$AM: A Single-Stream SAM with Reliability-Calibrated Frequency Adapter for Multi-modal Salient Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17475)
*Ruichao Hou,Boyue Xu,Tongwei Ren,Dongming Zhou,Gangshan Wu,Jinde Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于SAM的单流多模态显著目标检测框架，通过可靠性校准的频率适应模块控制辅助高频信息注入，仅用12.20M可训练参数（占总参数5.4%）即可在RGB-D、RGB-T和RGB-NIR基准上取得竞争性性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAM适配的MSOD方法多采用双流编码器或辅助提示生成器，导致计算冗余；而单流替代方案虽降低成本，但早期融合可能将噪声或错位的辅助高频线索传播到主干网络中，影响检测性能。

Method: 提出单流框架SSSAM：(1) 混合频率专家模块，利用平稳小波变换分解各模态并聚合跨模态频率信息；(2) 可靠性校准频率适配器，采用双门控校准机制，选择性地跨Transformer阶段传播校准残差，联合控制注入强度和跨模态可靠性；(3) 超网络引导的语义-结构解码器，结合主干网络的语义掩码特征与基于Mamba的结构细节恢复。

Result: 在RGB-D、RGB-T和RGB-NIR三个多模态显著目标检测基准上进行综合实验，框架取得了竞争性性能，仅需12.20M可训练参数，占总参数的5.4%。

Conclusion: 通过可靠性校准的频率适应机制，单流SAM框架能够避免重复基础编码器并显式控制辅助频率注入，在保证性能的同时显著降低可训练参数开销，为高效多模态显著目标检测提供了新思路。

Abstract: Vision foundation models have recently advanced multi-modal salient object detection (MSOD) through parameter-efficient tuning and prompt learning. However, existing Segment Anything Model (SAM)-adapted MSOD methods often rely on dual-stream encoders or auxiliary prompt generators, leading to redundant computation. Although a single-stream alternative can reduce this cost, early fusion may also propagate noisy or misaligned auxiliary high-frequency cues through the backbone. In this paper, we propose a novel single-stream framework that integrates reliability-calibrated frequency adaptation into the adopted SAM backbone for MSOD. It avoids duplicated foundation backbones while explicitly controlling auxiliary frequency injection. Specifically, we design a mixture of frequency experts module, which uses the stationary wavelet transform to decompose each modality and aggregate cross-modal frequency information. We further introduce a reliability-calibrated frequency adapter with a dual-gate calibration mechanism, which selectively propagates the calibrated residual across transformer stages while jointly controlling its injection strength and cross-modal reliability. A hypernetwork-guided semantic-structural decoder then combines semantic mask features from the adopted backbone with Mamba-based structural detail recovery. Comprehensive experiments on RGB-D, RGB-T, and RGB-NIR salient object detection benchmarks validate that the proposed framework achieves competitive performance with only 12.20M trainable parameters, accounting for 5.4\% of the total parameters. The code will be available at https://github.com/xuboyue1999/SSSAM.

</details>


### [40] [NeuroPath: Brain-Inspired Dual-Pathway Graph Convolutional Networks for Skeleton-Based Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17487)
*Kanglei Zhou,Ruizhi Cai,Hubert P. H. Shum,Frederick W. B. Li,Xiaohui Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种名为NeuroPath的双通道图卷积网络，受人类视觉腹侧和背侧通路启发，将骨骼动作识别中的空间和时间信息分别建模并协作融合，在多个基准数据集上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有STGCN通过隐式联合建模空间-时间表示，导致不同骨骼模态（结构特征与运动特征）之间性能不均衡，未能充分挖掘互补的结构和运动信息。

Method: 1) 采用双通道架构（仿生视觉腹侧/背侧通路），分别建模空间与时间信息；2) 通过转换单元将输入转为各通道特定的骨骼表示；3) 引入群图卷积块动态识别关键身体部位并建模其时空依赖；4) 通道间动态融合模块整合互补的跨模态信息以支持高层语义理解。

Result: 在Kinetics Skeleton 400、NTU RGB+D 60和NTU RGB+D 120三个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，性能持续提升，验证了双通道时空建模的有效性。

Conclusion: 分离但协作的双通道时空建模优于隐式时空耦合方法，为骨骼动作识别提供了一种受神经科学启发的有效架构。

Abstract: Skeleton-based action recognition aims to recognize human actions from sequences of human joint coordinates. Most existing Spatial-Temporal Graph Convolutional Networks (STGCNs) have achieved promising results by modeling skeletal structures with implicit spatial-temporal representations. However, our empirical study reveals a clear performance imbalance across different skeletal modalities, indicating that implicitly coupling spatial and temporal information limits the full exploitation of complementary structural and motion cues. Inspired by the ventral and dorsal pathways in human perception, we propose Dual-Pathway Graph Convolutional Networks (NeuroPath), which adopt a dual-pathway architecture for separate yet collaborative modeling of spatial and temporal information. Specifically, transformation units first convert the input into pathway-specific skeletal representations, allowing each pathway to focus on complementary aspects of human motion. To further capture coordinated joint behaviors and their interrelationships, we introduce a group graph convolution block that dynamically identifies key body parts and models their spatial-temporal dependencies. In addition, inter-pathway dynamic fusion modules integrate complementary inter-modal information across pathways, facilitating higher-level semantic interpretation of actions. Extensive experiments on Kinetics Skeleton 400, NTU RGB+D 60, and NTU RGB+D 120 demonstrate consistent performance improvements, validating the effectiveness of dual-pathway spatial-temporal modeling for skeleton-based action recognition.

</details>


### [41] [When More Foundation Models Means Less: Diagnosing and Addressing Multi-View Fusion Failure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17490)
*Yibo Liu,Bowen Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出KAGES方法，将基础模型集线器中的多视角融合问题形式化为视图集组合问题，通过核对齐贪心选择器从大规模异构编码器池中挑选紧凑且任务对齐的视图子集，在多种协议下相比全量融合和现有方法显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型集线器将多视角融合转化为选择问题，但现有研究未充分关注"融合多少个编码器、融合哪些编码器"这一核心问题。作者观察到下游性能随融合编码器数量呈非单调变化——后添加的视图可能冗余或任务不对齐，导致精度饱和甚至下降。这促使需要一种有效的视图选择方法，而非盲目融合更多编码器。

Method: 论文将多视角融合形式化为视图集组合问题，提出KAGES（基于核对齐的贪心编码器选择器）。该方法以标签感知的方式，通过计算各冻结编码器对中心化核-目标对齐（centred kernel-target alignment）的边际增益对其进行排序。KAGES在选择过程中无需训练下游分类器，每个候选评估时间复杂度为O(n²)，与编码器维度无关，并在单调性和正子模比率条件下提供条件性(1-e^{-γ})前缀级保证。

Result: 在五个识别场景及低样本、更大池、全数据三种协议下，KAGES相比全量融合在平均AULC上分别提升3.9、5.8和3.3个百分点，并超过DPP和设施位置选择方法。图像检索呈现沿KAGES排序的后期任务相关饱和现象，冻结LLM融合中重现了先升后降的模式。

Conclusion: 有效的大规模池融合依赖选择紧凑、任务对齐的视图集合，而非无差别地融合更多编码器。KAGES为这一选择问题提供了高效、有理论保证的解决方案。

Abstract: Foundation-model hubs turn multi-view fusion into a selection problem: from a large heterogeneous encoder pool, which views should be fused, and how many? We show that downstream performance is non-monotonic in the number of fused encoders; later views can be redundant or task-misaligned, causing accuracy to saturate or decline. We formalise this setting as view-set composition and propose KAGES (Kernel-Alignment Greedy Encoder Selector), a label-aware method that orders frozen encoders by their marginal gain in centred kernel-target alignment. KAGES requires no downstream classifier training during selection, evaluates each candidate in $\mathcal{O}(n^2)$ time independent of encoder dimension, and admits a conditional $(1-e^{-γ})$ prefix-wise guarantee under monotonicity and a positive submodularity ratio. Across five recognition regimes and low-shot, larger-pool, and full-data protocols, KAGES improves average AULC over full fusion by 3.9, 5.8, and 3.3 points, respectively, and exceeds DPP and facility-location selection in average AULC. Image retrieval exhibits later, task-dependent saturation along the KAGES ordering, while peak-then-decline reproduces in frozen-LLM fusion. These results show that effective large-pool fusion depends on selecting a compact, task-aligned set of views rather than indiscriminately fusing more encoders.

</details>


### [42] [SE-MoLoRA: Shared-Expert LoRA Adapters for Domain-Specific Photographic Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17514)
*Bishwash Khanal,Anlan Zhang,Sasu Tarkoma,Tommi Mikkonen,Abhishek Kumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SE-MoLoRA，一种用于摄影领域审美评估的模块化参数高效适配框架。它通过共享LoRA专家和路由的专业适配器分离摄影通用知识与多领域残差判断，在保留较少激活参数的同时显著提升评论生成的针对性和可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型虽然能流畅描述图像，但往往无法提供可操作的摄影批评，因为语义内容与审美判断在表示中相互纠缠。现有方法无法分离领域特定的审美知识，且训练多个独立专家模型成本过高。

Method: SE-MoLoRA包含两部分：一个始终激活的rank-64共享LoRA适配器捕获通用摄影词汇，以及在构图、光照和技术质量三个子领域上路由的rank-32专业适配器，用于学习领域残差。轻量查询路由器选择相关专业适配器，并通过正交正则化惩罚鼓励专家表示去纠缠。训练数据通过蒸馏Reddit摄影批评数据集为带领域标签的批评样本获得。

Result: 在保留的批评生成任务上，SE-MoLoRA将BERTScore-F1从monolithic LoRA的0.2317提升到0.4215，在84.6%的两两比较中被偏好，同时激活参数少于独立的专家模型。SVD消融研究表明共享-专家分解和正交正则化有效减少了专家重叠。

Conclusion: 模块化适配能够提升多模态摄影批评的可控性和针对性，表明分离共享知识与领域残差是一种有效的参数高效适配策略。

Abstract: Vision-language models can describe images fluently, but they often fail to provide actionable photographic critique because semantic content and aesthetic judgment remain entangled. We propose SE-MoLoRA, a modular parameter-efficient adaptation framework for domain-specific photographic assessment. The method separates general photographic knowledge from specialist residual judgments using an always-active shared LoRA expert and routed adapters for composition, lighting, and technical quality. A lightweight query router selects the relevant specialist, enabling targeted critique without training separate full models. A rank-64 shared adapter captures broad photographic vocabulary, while rank-32 specialists learn domain-specific residuals with an orthogonal regularization penalty that encourages disentangled representations. Training data is obtained by distilling the Reddit Photo Critique Dataset into domain-labeled critique samples. On held-out critique generation, SE-MoLoRA improves BERTScore-F1 from 0.2317 to 0.4215 over monolithic LoRA and is preferred in 84.6\% of pairwise comparisons, while using fewer active parameters than separate specialist models. SVD-based ablation study shows that shared-specialist decomposition and orthogonal regularization reduce expert overlap. These results demonstrate that modular adaptation improves controllability and specificity in multimodal photographic critique.

</details>


### [43] [Looking Beyond the Scale: Do Surgical Skill Models Learn Transferable Representations Across Assessment Rubrics?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17519)
*Hanna Hoffmann,Felix von Bechtolsheim,Stefanie Speidel,Rebecca Hisey*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了基于视觉的手术技能评估模型在GOALS和OSATS两种评分标准、LASANA和JIGSAWS两个数据集之间的跨域迁移能力。研究发现JIGSAWS预训练骨干在LASANA上取得0.77-0.80的CCC值，接近端到端基线，表明跨评分标准迁移可行；但反向迁移到JIGSAWS则完全失败，可能源于注释不一致。对照实验表明任务特定头承担了大部分技能预测负担，而骨干网络仅需提供足够的时空特征即可。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉的手术技能评估模型仅在单一数据集上取得良好表现，但从未系统探究这些模型学到的是可迁移的技能表征还是特定于数据集的视觉模式。这一根本性问题尚未被提出和研究。

Method: 使用三种针对性诊断方法：(1)端到端训练测试监督技能学习是否直接迁移；(2)自适应锐度感知最小化(ASAM)探究平坦损失曲面是否提升泛化能力；(3)基于增强的自监督和对比学习检验域不变预训练是否能将技能与视觉上下文解耦。采用JIGSAWS的非重叠参与者留出测试集进行双向迁移评估。

Result: 迁移结果存在不对称性：JIGSAWS预训练骨干在LASANA上CCC值为0.77-0.80，接近端到端基线，证明跨评分标准迁移可行；但向JIGSAWS的迁移在所有方法下均失败，可能归因于注释不一致。Kinetics预训练骨干的对照实验表明任务特定头承担了大部分预测负担。

Conclusion: 视觉成分在技能预测中占主导地位但并非唯一因素。技能表征能否跨评分系统迁移这一核心问题此前从未被研究，本文提供了初步视角，但需进一步工作来明确区分可迁移的技能特征与绑定于特定视觉域的特征。

Abstract: Vision-based surgical skill assessment has shown strong in-domain results, yet a fundamental question remains unasked: do these models learn transferable representations of surgical proficiency, or do they merely encode dataset-specific visual patterns?
  This paper systematically analyzes what limits cross-domain skill transfer between the GOALS and OSATS assessment scales using the LASANA and JIGSAWS datasets. Each evaluated method serves a targeted diagnostic purpose: end-to-end training to test whether supervised skill learning transfers directly, Adaptive Sharpness-Aware Minimization (ASAM) to probe whether flatter loss landscapes improve generalization, and augmentation-based self-supervised and contrastive learning to assess whether domain-invariant pretraining decouples skill from visual context. Transfer is evaluated in both directions using a disjoint-participant held-out test set for JIGSAWS.
  Results reveal an asymmetry: backbones pretrained on JIGSAWS achieve CCC values of 0.77 to 0.80 on LASANA, closely matching the end-to-end baseline, showing cross-rubric transfer is feasible when the target domain provides consistent supervision. Transfer to JIGSAWS fails across all methods, likely due to annotation inconsistencies. Control experiments with a Kinetics-pretrained backbone suggest task-specific heads carry the majority of the skill prediction burden, while the backbone need only provide adequate spatiotemporal features.
  These findings offer a new perspective on vision-based skill assessment: the central question of whether skill representations transfer across scoring systems has not been previously investigated. Results indicate the visual component is dominant but not solely responsible for skill prediction; further work is needed to conclusively disentangle transferable skill features from those bound to a specific visual domain.

</details>


### [44] [BrainNorm: A Foundation Model that knows Normal via Semantic Atlas Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17521)
*Madhumitha Venkatesh,Shanawaj S Madarkar,Konda Reddy Mopuri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BrainNorm，一个基于约66,000张T1加权结构MRI扫描训练的规范性基础模型。通过语言-图像风格对比预训练，在健康队列中学习语义图谱潜在空间（SAL），将每张扫描表示为图谱分区嵌入集合，从而支持年龄一致的模板匹配和局部化偏差评分。在6个下游队列、25种任务-设置组合中验证了泛化能力，覆盖年龄估计、脑龄差距估计、分区识别及单/多疾病分类，支持直接推理、零样本、少样本和全数据线性探针设置。在冻结嵌入上的线性探针优于9个端到端微调基线，且识别的局部化偏差与临床文献中的神经退行性病理高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经影像基础模型缺乏能够同时支持年龄规范建模、局部化偏差评分和多疾病分类的统一框架。需要一个能够在健康队列上学习规范性衰老轨迹，并将个体偏差映射到特定脑区的模型，以实现可解释、零样本友好的疾病预测。

Method: BrainNorm采用语言-图像风格的对比预训练方法，在健康人群的约66,000张T1w sMRI上训练。其核心是学习语义图谱潜在空间（SAL），将每张扫描编码为一组图谱分区嵌入。该空间可生成分区特异性的健康衰老模板轨迹，支持年龄一致的模板匹配和相对年龄的局部化偏差评分。下游适应采用冻结嵌入上的线性探针，可选健康队列微调。

Result: 在6个下游队列、25种任务-设置组合中，BrainNorm在年龄估计、脑龄差距估计、分区识别、单/多疾病分类任务上表现出色。冻结嵌入上的线性探针在所有分类任务中优于9个端到端微调基线。其识别的局部化偏差模式与临床文献中神经退行性疾病的已知病理高度一致。

Conclusion: BrainNorm证明了规范性基础模型在神经影像领域（特别是结构性MRI）中的潜力：通过学习健康的衰老轨迹并在潜在空间中进行局部化偏差检测，可以在无需大量标注监督的情况下实现多疾病分类和可解释的病理定位，为临床辅助诊断提供了有力工具。

Abstract: We introduce BrainNorm, a normative foundation model, trained and tested on ~66,000 T1-weighted structural MRI (T1w sMRI) scans. By leveraging language-image style contrastive pretraining on healthy cohorts across ages, BrainNorm learns a Semantic Atlas Latent space (SAL), where each scan is represented as a set of atlas-parcel embeddings. This yields parcel-specific healthy aging template trajectories that support age-consistent template matching and localized deviation scoring relative to a subject's chronological age. Across 6 downstream cohorts, BrainNorm demonstrates generalization evaluated across 25 task-setting combinations spanning age estimation, brain-age gap estimation, parcel identification, and single- & multi-disease classification tasks under direct inference, zero-shot, few-shot & full-data linear-probe settings. The resulting deviation patterns in SAL space enable zero-shot tasks for disease prediction using parcel-wise abnormalities. Fine-tuning on healthy-only cohorts of downstream datasets further improves the performance of various tasks. Across all classification tasks, linear probing on BrainNorm's frozen embeddings outperforms 9 baselines finetuned under end-to-end supervision. Furthermore, the localized deviations identified by BrainNorm across various neurodegenerative disorders closely align with established neurodegeneration pathology in clinical literature.

</details>


### [45] [Explainable AI-Powered Framework for Video-Based Skill Assessment in Cataract Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17522)
*Mohammad Javad Ahmadi,Hamid D. Taghirad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种可解释的AI框架，用于白内障手术技能的自动化评估，并引入了包含2000段白内障手术视频的世界最大数据集，以及新的Capsulorhexis Skill Assessment System (CSAS)主观评分系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外科劳动力持续短缺且传统训练方法存在固有局限性，因此亟需自动化、数据驱动的方法来改进外科教育。

Method: 采用先进的计算机视觉和信号处理技术自动评估手术视频，提取十种基于运动的客观指标，并与新引入的CSAS主观评分系统进行对比验证；框架强调输出的可解释性，而非仅仅作为黑箱技能分类工具。

Result: 通过对83段白内障手术视频的实验分析，自动计算的指标与专家主观评分之间具有强相关性，手术技能评估准确率高达87%。

Conclusion: 自动评估指标与专家主观评分之间的稳健相关性表明该框架能够准确建模外科手术专业水平，可补充甚至替代传统的主观评分方法。

Abstract: Persistent shortages in the surgical workforce and inherent limitations of traditional training methods highlight the necessity of automated, data-driven approaches in surgical education. This study addresses these challenges by introducing a novel, explainable AI-powered framework for automated skill assessment, specifically focusing on cataract surgery. We present the world's largest dataset of cataract surgery videos, comprising 2,000 recordings. Additionally, we propose an AI-powered analytical framework that employs advanced computer vision and signal-processing techniques to automatically evaluate surgical videos to derive objective, quantitative performance indicators that complement or potentially replace subjective scoring methods. A significant advantage of our framework over previous methods lies precisely in its explainability of outputs, elevating it beyond merely an opaque skill classification tool. Through experimental analysis of 83 cataract surgery videos, we demonstrate that the automatically computed metrics exhibit strong correlations with expert-based subjective evaluations, achieving up to 87% accuracy in surgical skill assessment. Each metric was individually examined, and expert surgeons provided subjective ratings using the newly introduced Capsulorhexis Skill Assessment System (CSAS). These subjective assessments were compared with ten objective motion-based metrics extracted through our framework. The results indicated a robust correlation between subjective ratings and automated indicators, underscoring the framework's capacity to accurately model surgical expertise.

</details>


### [46] [GroupForward: Building Referable 3D Scenes via Instance-Grouped Feed-Forward Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17535)
*Qijian Tian,Zimeng Wu,Xuhong Wang,Lizhuang Ma,Xin Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GroupForward，一个实例分组的正向高斯泼溅（feed-forward 3DGS）模型，从稀疏、无位姿、无标定的多视角图像中同时重建几何、外观、实例结构和语义信息。不同于现有方法为每个高斯附加高维语义特征，GroupForward学习紧凑的实例嵌入，将高斯分组为跨视角一致的3D实例，并在此基础上提出引用性场景推理框架（RSRF），通过构建实例分组的3D场景图并结合视觉语言模型实现复杂的3D引用分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈语义3DGS方法缺乏显式的实例判别能力，主要支持基于类别或短语的语义查询，无法实现复杂的引用性场景推理（如识别"红色杯子旁边的蓝色椅子"）。

Method: 1) GroupForward：学习紧凑实例嵌入而非逐高斯高维语义特征，将高斯分组为跨视角一致的3D实例，将语义3DGS从逐高斯特征渲染重构为实例级语义聚合与传播；2) RSRF（引用性场景推理框架）：构建实例分组3D场景图，检索候选实例，利用视觉语言模型基于结构化实例证据和多视角观测进行推理，识别被引用实例。

Result: 在语义重建和引用推理实验上验证了实例分组重建与推理框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过实例分组建模和引用性场景推理框架，将3D场景理解从简单语义查询扩展到复杂引用推理，为具身智能体提供了更完整的场景理解能力。

Abstract: Simultaneously reconstructing and understanding 3D environments is essential for embodied agents. Toward this goal, feed-forward semantic 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) efficiently constructs semantic scene representations from sparse multi-view observations. However, existing methods lack explicit instance discrimination and mainly support category- or phrase-based semantic queries. To this end, we propose GroupForward, an instance-grouped feed-forward Gaussian splatting model that reconstructs geometry, appearance, instance structure, and semantics from sparse, unposed, and uncalibrated multi-view images. Unlike existing methods that attach high-dimensional semantic features to each Gaussian, GroupForward learns compact instance embeddings that group Gaussians into cross-view consistent 3D instances, reformulating feed-forward semantic 3DGS from per-Gaussian semantic feature rendering to instance-level semantic aggregation and propagation. Building on these instance groups, we further propose a Referential Scene Reasoning Framework (RSRF) for complex 3D referring segmentation. RSRF constructs an instance-grouped 3D scene graph and retrieves candidate instances for a given referring expression. A vision-language model then reasons over structured instance evidence and multi-view observations to identify the referred instance among the candidates. RSRF thereby extends language interaction from simple semantic querying to complex referential scene reasoning. Experiments on semantic reconstruction and referential reasoning demonstrate the effectiveness of our instance-grouped reconstruction and reasoning framework.

</details>


### [47] [Code as Representation: A Compilable Parsing Paradigm for Academic Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17550)
*Rihui Jin,Jun Wang,chengyuan zhu,Liang Mingyu,Yue Gao,Li Yunxuan,Kuicai Dong,Guilin Qi,Lin Ren,Yongrui Chen,Xinbang Dai,Jiaqi Li,Tongtong Wu,Gholamreza Haffari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出可编译学术文档解析(CADP)范式，将学术PDF页面重建为上下文相关的LaTeX加可执行Python代码，并构建CADP-Bench基准和重新注入编译协议来评估多模态大语言模型在结构感知科学文档解析上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术论文是科学知识的主要载体，但现有PDF格式以人类阅读而非机器使用为优化目标。对多模态大语言模型(MLLM)而言，核心挑战不只是感知，更是表征问题：科学页面交错包含表格、公式、图表和伪代码等结构化学术元素(SAE)，这些元素的结构、数据和逻辑在Markdown等常见替代格式中难以被保真地表示，导致机器无法有效理解和重建。

Method: 提出CADP范式，将完整页面重建为上下文相关LaTeX加可执行Python代码，使得结构保持元素和可执行图表表示能够被重建、重新编译并直接对照源页面验证。构建CADP-Bench，一个专家验证的完整学术页面基准，包含紧密耦合的文本和多种SAE类型，采用重新注入编译协议评估；并使用SOTA MLLM和整合常见智能体技术的探索性多智能体基线进行研究。

Result: 实验结果显示，即使是前沿模型在生成高保真可执行重建方面仍然困难重重，暴露了结构感知科学文档解析仍有很大的改进空间。

Conclusion: 当前MLLM在CADP任务上的表现表明结构感知科学文档解析仍需大量改进，CADP-Bench已公开发布以支持未来研究。

Abstract: Academic papers are a primary carrier of scientific knowledge, yet most of this knowledge remains locked in PDFs that are optimized for human reading rather than machine use. For Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), the core challenge is not only perception, but representation: scientific pages interleave text with Structured Academic Elements (SAEs) such as tables, formulas, charts, and pseudocode, whose structure, data, and logic are poorly preserved by common surrogates like Markdown. We therefore propose Compilable Academic Document Parsing (CADP), a paradigm that reconstructs a full page as contextual \LaTeX{} plus executable Python, so that structure-preserving elements and executable chart representations can be reconstructed, recompiled, and directly verified against the source page. To support this setting, we introduce CADP-Bench, an expert-verified benchmark of full academic pages containing tightly coupled text and multiple SAE types, evaluated through a re-injection compilation protocol. We further study current capabilities using SOTA MLLMs and an exploratory multi-agent baseline that incorporates common agentic techniques. Results show that even frontier models still struggle to produce high-fidelity executable reconstructions, highlighting substantial room for improvement in structure-aware scientific document parsing. CADP-Bench is released for future research.

</details>


### [48] [MSEditor: Toward Consistent Multi-Shot Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17559)
*Kunyu Feng,Yue Ma,Bingyuan Wang,Yuefeng Wang,Zhiyuan Qin,Hao Cheng,Hao Li,Qifeng Chen,Zeyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MSEditor是首个专门针对多镜头视频一致编辑的框架，通过重利用多视角视频数据集作为跨镜头监督信号，引入Supervisory Adapter注入跨镜头信息，并提出Cross-Shot Packing策略在自注意力窗口中动态聚合语义相关片段的信息，以解决多镜头视频编辑中的身份漂移和累积误差传播问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多镜头视频由不连续的时间片段组成，各片段在视角、相机尺度和主体姿态上差异显著，导致身份漂移和累积错误传播。实现一致性编辑需要可靠的跨镜头语义感知来维持主体外观的稳定性和视觉连续性，但现有方法无法有效解决这一挑战。

Method: 提出MSEditor框架：(1) 将现有多个视角的视频数据集重新利用为跨镜头监督信号，克服高质量多镜头训练数据稀缺的问题；(2) 引入Supervisory Adapter，将跨镜头信息注入扩散骨干网络，使模型学习身份一致性的表示；(3) 设计Cross-Shot Packing策略，在自注意力窗口内动态聚合语义相关片段的信息，以缓解累积误差并保证长程时间一致性。

Result: 在自建的多镜头视频编辑基准上，MSEditor在身份保持、时间稳定性和整体视觉质量方面显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: MSEditor通过跨镜头监督和动态信息聚合有效解决了多镜头视频编辑中的身份漂移和误差累积问题，为该领域提供了首个专门的解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we tackle the problem of performing consistent, unified modifications to a multi-shot video sequence. This task is particularly challenging because multi-shot videos consist of discontinuous temporal segments that vary significantly in viewpoint, camera scale, and subject pose, leading to severe identity drift and cumulative error propagation. Achieving coherent edits requires establishing reliable cross-shot semantic awareness to maintain stable subject appearance and visual continuity across these disjointed boundaries. To address this, we propose MSEditor, the first framework designed specifically for consistent multi-shot video editing. To overcome the scarcity of high-quality multi-shot training data, we repurpose existing multi-view video datasets to provide robust cross-shot supervision. Architecturally, we introduce a Supervisory Adapter that injects this cross-shot information into the diffusion backbone, enabling the model to learn identity-consistent representations. Furthermore, to effectively mitigate cumulative errors and ensure long-range temporal coherence, we design a Cross-Shot Packing strategy that dynamically aggregates information from semantically related shots within the self-attention window. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MSEditor significantly outperforms existing methods on our curated multi-shot video editing benchmark in terms of identity preservation, temporal stability, and overall visual quality.

</details>


### [49] [Leveraging existing sparse point annotations for benthic imagery dense segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17561)
*Cesar Borja,Breck A. McCollum,Jarret E. Byrnes,Kenneth Sebens,Ana C. Murillo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用历史稀疏专家点标注作为视觉提示（prompt）驱动SAM2模型自动生成高质量伪标签的方法，通过自动过滤不可靠的标注点，训练细粒度语义分割模型，实现海洋底栖生态系统的规模化监测与分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋生态系统的健康监测对全球环境变化研究至关重要，但水下观测的物理限制和海洋图像处理的固有挑战严重制约了系统化监测的可扩展性。虽然SAM系列视觉基础模型表现出色，但在复杂水下场景中仍难以进行细粒度识别，且需要专家监督。同时，历史底栖调查数据通常只用少量稀疏专家点标注，这些珍贵的弱监督信息未被充分利用。

Method: 本文的核心创新在于：1）将历史底栖调查中的稀疏专家点标注作为SAM2的视觉提示（point prompts）；2）设计了一种自动识别机制，判断哪些历史标注点在传播过程中是可靠的、哪些是有害的，通过过滤不可靠的标注点，提取高质量伪真实掩码（pseudo-ground-truth masks）；3）利用这些伪标签训练更精确的细粒度语义分割模型。

Result: 在公开底栖数据上验证了方法的有效性，并引入了一个新的、具有挑战性的基准数据集，该数据集包含真实世界的稀疏专家标注，为可扩展的生态分析提供了基础。

Conclusion: 本文通过桥接先进视觉基础模型与现存的稀疏监督信息，实现了一种无需额外专家介入即可获取高质量细粒度分割标签的机制，为海洋底栖生态系统的规模化自动监测与分析开辟了可行路径。

Abstract: The health of marine ecosystems is a critical indicator of global environmental change, yet the physical constraints of underwater observation and the intrinsic challenges of processing marine imagery severely limit the scalability of systematic monitoring. While recent visual foundation models such as the Segment Anything Model (SAM) series show great promise, they still struggle with the fine-grained recognition required in these complex scenarios and still require expert supervision. Our work addresses this gap by bridging state-of-the-art foundation models with existing sparse supervision. Because historical benthic surveys are typically annotated with only a few sparse expert points per image, we utilize these legacy point-labels as visual prompts for SAM2. Our primary contribution is a novel mechanism to automatically identify which of these points are suitable, and which are actively harmful, when used for propagation. By filtering out unreliable points, we extract high-quality pseudo-ground-truth masks capable of training more accurate, fine-grained semantic segmentation models. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach on public benthic data and introduce a new, challenging benchmark featuring real-world sparse expert annotations, paving the way for scalable ecological analysis.

</details>


### [50] [Where a New Concept Must Enter: Entry Point Gates Cross-Task Usability in Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17564)
*Zongyang Qiu,Yihan Wu,Kaixuan Fan,Bo Li,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过构造性分离方法研究统一多模态模型（UMM）中理解与生成两个方向的关系，发现双向通道均真实存在但性质不同：生成训练安装的"名称"只能匹配候选，理解训练安装的"名称"还能生成。跨任务可用性由绑定进入共享计算的位置决定，对齐探针可预测36种配置的导出效果（Spearman ρ=+0.68）。在28层模型中，激活空间的对齐注入在7层可实现概念绘制、14层后与基础模型无异，而权重版本的对齐编辑在10-14层最优。观测四个模型后发现，只有理解路径为语义视觉编码器时才出现此窗口，表明统一权重不够，两个方向必须在入口点共享语义格式。利用该规则，中间栈对齐目标仅以0.1%的通用文生图能力相对损失获取概念，而标准生成路径损失高达41%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态模型（UMMs）的核心理念是理解与生成相互促进，但受控消融实验反复发现添加生成目标后理解能力没有提升。联合训练研究无法解决这一争议，因为重叠监督下无法区分增益来自架构还是数据。为深入探究UMMs中两个方向的真实关系，作者通过构造性设计将其分离。

Method: 设计一种新型视觉实体：将渲染的3D资产与一个经筛选的伪词配对（确保该词在冻结模型中不存在），仅通过单一任务方向绑定该实体，然后测量未训练方向的表现。此外引入对齐探针（alignment probe）预测跨任务可用性，并在激活空间和权重空间分别进行对齐编辑实验，对比不同注入层对概念可绘制性的影响。

Result: 研究发现：理解与生成之间的通道在双向都真实存在但性质不同——生成训练安装的名称只能匹配候选，理解训练安装的名称还能生成。跨任务可用性由绑定进入共享计算的位置决定，对齐探针在36种配置上预测导出效果达Spearman ρ=+0.68。激活空间的闭式对齐注入在28层模型的第7层可使概念可绘制，从第14层起与基础模型无法区分；权重版本编辑峰值在10-14层。在中栈使用对齐目标获取概念仅损失0.1%的通用文生图能力，标准生成路径则损失41%。

Conclusion: 统一权重不足以保证理解与生成方向的相互促进，两个方向必须在入口点共享语义格式。只有理解路径为语义视觉编码器时才出现有效窗口，表明架构设计的语义对齐比简单权重统一更为关键。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) are motivated by the hope that understanding and generation reinforce each other but controlled ablations repeatedly find that adding a generation objective leaves understanding flat. Joint-training studies cannot settle the disagreement: with overlapping supervision, a gain cannot be attributed to the architecture rather than the data. To further investigate the relationship between the two directions in UMMs, we separate them by construction. A novel visual entity, a rendered 3D asset paired with a pseudo-word screened for absence from the frozen model's behavior, is bound through exactly one task direction, and the untrained direction is then measured. We find that the channel is real in both directions, but the directions differ in kind: generation training installs a name the model can only match among candidates; understanding training installs one it can also produce. What governs cross-task usability is where the binding enters the shared computation. An alignment probe predicts export across 36 configurations (Spearman $ρ= +0.68$). That objective's alignment term, maximized in closed form over activations with every weight frozen, makes a concept drawable when injected at layer 7 of 28 and is indistinguishable from the base model from layer 14 on, while the weight-based version of the same edit peaks at layers 10-14. In an observational series of four models, this window appears only where the understanding pathway is a semantic vision encoder, suggesting that unified weights are not enough: the two directions must share a semantic format at the entry point. Exploiting the rule, a mid-stack alignment objective acquires the concept for a $0.1\%$ relative loss of the model's general text-to-image ability, against $41\%$ for the standard generative route. Our code is at https://github.com/Zane-ZYQiu/entry-point-umm.

</details>


### [51] [CoinVE-200K: A Large-Scale High-Quality Dataset for Compositional Instruction-Guided Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17566)
*Fuchen Long,Cong Wang,Zitao Gao,Wenhao Zhong,Yu Cheng,Xiaolu Hou,Yan Li,Xiao Cao,Xinlong Sun,Xi Chen,Yu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向组合式指令视频编辑的大规模高质量数据集CoinVE-200K（含1080p、最多201帧、每条样本包含2-5个原子编辑操作），配合评测基准CoinVE-Bench和基于Wan2.1-T2V-14B与Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct构建的22B组合式视频编辑模型CoinVE-Edit，实现多区域、多指令的联合理解与精确编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令视频编辑数据集主要聚焦单一编辑操作，无法支持组合式指令引导的视频编辑——即在同一视频中需要联合理解并忠实执行多个编辑意图。目前缺乏大规模、高质量的数据集来支撑这一任务的研究与发展。

Method: 1) 构建CoinVE-200K：通过精心设计的生成和过滤流水线，构建1080p、最高201帧的高质量组合式视频编辑数据集，每条样本涵盖2-5个原子编辑操作，指令面向人、物、背景，编辑类型包括增删改和风格化。2) 提出CoinVE-Bench基准，覆盖不同主体、操作类型和指令复杂度。3) 提出CoinVE-Edit，一个22B的组合式视频编辑模型，基于Wan2.1-T2V-14B和Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct构建，通过解耦不同编辑指令的区域感知注意力，实现精确的多区域编辑，同时保持无关内容与时间一致性。

Result: 在CoinVE-Bench上的实验表明，CoinVE-Edit在指令遵循、组合编辑准确性、视觉质量和时间一致性方面均取得了强劲的表现。

Conclusion: 本研究填补了组合式指令视频编辑领域的空白，通过大规模高质量数据集、标准化基准和专用模型三个维度，系统性地推动了组合式指令视频编辑任务的发展，验证了解耦区域感知注意力机制在复杂多指令视频编辑中的有效性。

Abstract: The quality and diversity of instruction-based video editing datasets are steadily improving, yet existing datasets mainly focus on single editing operations and fall short in supporting compositional instruction-guided video editing. In particular, multiple editing intents must be jointly understood and faithfully executed within the same video. To address this issue, we introduce CoinVE-200K, a large-scale, high-quality dataset for Compositional Instruction-Guided Video Editing. CoinVE-200K contains 1080p video-editing pairs of up to 201 frames, covering diverse compositional scenarios where each sample involves 2 to 5 atomic editing operations. The instructions target humans, objects, and backgrounds, and cover edit types such as addition, removal, modification, and stylization. All samples are built through a carefully designed generation and filtering pipeline to ensure instruction faithfulness, visual quality, temporal consistency, and compositional diversity. We also introduce CoinVE-Bench, a benchmark for compositional-instruction video editing across diverse subjects, operation types, and instruction complexities. Furthermore, we present CoinVE-Edit, a 22B compositional video editing model built upon Wan2.1-T2V-14B and Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct. CoinVE-Edit disentangles region-aware attention for different editing instructions, enabling precise multi-region editing while preserving irrelevant content and temporal coherence. Experiments on CoinVE-Bench show that CoinVE-Edit achieves strong performance in instruction following, compositional editing accuracy, visual quality, and temporal consistency.

</details>


### [52] [SpurCon: Weighted Supervised Contrastive Learning for Mitigating Spurious Cues in Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17598)
*Shenhav Nadir,Meir Yossef Levi,Eyal Gofer,Guy Gilboa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SpurCon，一个轻量级框架，通过新的监督对比损失（WtSupCon）利用元数据和预测的虚假标签来缓解医学图像分类中对虚假相关性的依赖，无需网络训练即可通过少量专家标注样本估计虚假标签，在多个数据集上实现了最佳的虚假缓解性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在医学影像中可能利用虚假相关性（如设备或治疗伪影与病理共现），在小型或类别不平衡数据集中进一步恶化最差组性能并削弱临床信任。现有方法需要领域知识来识别虚假线索，且难以在不牺牲整体精度的情况下缓解模型对这些线索的依赖。

Method: 提出SpurCon框架，包含两部分：(1) 一种无需网络训练的快速少样本流程，利用少量专家标注样本估计虚假标签；(2) 加权监督对比目标WtSupCon，根据病理、虚假标签和元数据的组合分配样本特定权重，使具有相同元数据和病理但仅虚假标签不同的样本获得高度相似的表征。方法基于预训练图像编码器（如BiomedCLIP），仅训练轻量级投影头。

Result: 在合成环境、Waterbirds、CheXpert（胸部X光分类）、ISIC 2020（皮肤癌分类）等多个数据集上评估，SpurCon在虚假缓解性能上表现最佳，在多个数据集上很好地平衡了最差组准确率和整体准确率。

Conclusion: SpurCon通过元数据和预测的虚假标签结合加权监督对比学习，在不训练完整网络的情况下有效缓解了医学图像分类中的虚假相关性问题，提高了模型的鲁棒性和临床可信度。

Abstract: Despite the rapid progress of deep neural networks in visual recognition, their adoption in high-risk medical applications remains limited due to reliability and robustness concerns. Models may exploit spurious correlations, particularly in medical imaging, where devices or treatment artifacts often co-occur with pathology. In small or imbalanced datasets, such cues further reduce worst-group performance and undermine clinical trust. To solve these issues, two major challenges should be addressed: identifying dataset-specific spurious cues, which typically require domain knowledge, and mitigating reliance on them. To tackle both, we propose SpurCon, a lightweight framework based on a novel supervised contrastive loss formulation that leverages available metadata and predicted spurious labels to enhance robustness. We introduce a fast few-shot procedure, without network training, to estimate spurious labels using a small number of expert-annotated samples. We then propose a weighted supervised contrastive objective, WtSupCon, that reshapes the representation geometry by assigning sample-specific weights that depend on the [pathology, spurious, metadata] combination. For example, the highest weight is assigned to samples that differ only in their spurious label. This yields highly similar representations for images with the same metadata and pathology, differing only in the predicted spurious label. Our method operates on pretrained image encoders (such as BiomedCLIP) and trains only a lightweight projection head. We evaluate SpurCon on a synthetic setting and on Waterbirds, CheXpert, a chest X-ray classification dataset, and ISIC 2020, a skin cancer classification dataset. Our approach delivers the best spurious-mitigation performance, balancing well worst-group and overall accuracy on multiple datasets.

</details>


### [53] [PathoArgus: Advancing Evidence-Grounded Long-Context Visual Reasoning across Gigapixel Whole-Slide and Multi-Slide Case Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17607)
*Bowen Liu,Qixiang Zhang,Xiaomeng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PathoArgus-Bench基准和PathoArgus阅读器，用于系统地测试全切片病理学推理中的证据链（可用性、可访问性、使用和响应性），发现即使GPT-5.6达到57%的准确率，在ESG（证据状态四元组）测试中仅有3.93%的四元组完全正确，证明行级准确率不能代表可靠的证据锚定推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有病理学问答基准仅测量最终答案准确率，这容易受到语言先验和基准规律的影响，无法证明预测确实基于提供的组织切片证据。需要显式评估整个证据链，以确保模型的推理真正锚定在输入的WSI图像上。

Method: 构建PathoArgus-Bench，包含22,078个四选一问题（来自4,913位患者、15个TCGA项目），覆盖六种病理学能力和三种证据需求层级，在固定阅读器预算下运行。引入ESG（Evidence State Quartets）受控测试集，固定问题文本但移动/替换/移除目标WSI集，要求跨状态预测一致。同时提出PathoArgus阅读器，通过问题相关性和空间覆盖分配上下文预算。评估20个通用、医学和病理学专用系统。

Result: GPT-5.6总体准确率57.09%、ESG准确率57.04%，但483个四元组中仅正确完成19个（QExact 3.93%）。PathoArgus阅读器达到50.39%总体准确率但QExact仅1.86%。两者均显示行级准确性无法转化为可靠的证据锚定推理。

Conclusion: 获取有用的全切片上下文是必要条件但远非充分条件。应将计算病理学的评估从答案中心转向证据锚定，关注预测是否真正基于提供的组织证据。

Abstract: Whole-slide pathology reasoning requires models to integrate gigapixel-scale visual evidence across complete case-linked slides, yet current question-answering benchmarks primarily measure final answer accuracy--a metric vulnerable to linguistic priors and benchmark regularities, and insufficient to establish that predictions are grounded in the supplied tissue. We introduce PathoArgus-Bench, a benchmark and evaluation protocol that explicitly tests the full evidence chain: availability, accessibility, use, and responsiveness. PathoArgus-Bench comprises 22,078 four-choice questions from 4,913 patients across 15 TCGA projects, covering six pathology capabilities across three levels of evidence demand, and operates under a fixed reader budget that retains only a small fraction of the gigapixel context. To further isolate evidence-grounded reasoning, we contribute ESG (Evidence State Quartets), a controlled set of 483 quartets where the question text is fixed while the target WSI set is moved, replaced, or removed, requiring consistent predictions across all states. Evaluating 20 general-purpose, medical, and pathology-specific systems reveals a stark gap: while GPT-5.6 achieves 57.09% overall accuracy and 57.04% on ESG, it correctly completes only 19 of 483 quartets (3.93% QExact), exposing that row-level accuracy does not translate into reliable evidence grounding. We also introduce PathoArgus, a fixed-budget reader that allocates context via question relevance and spatial coverage, attaining 50.39% overall accuracy yet only 1.86% QExact--demonstrating that improved context access alone does not ensure consistent evidence-based prediction. Our benchmark and diagnostics establish that acquiring useful whole-slide context is necessary but far from sufficient, and call for a shift from answer-centric to evidence-grounded evaluation in computational pathology.

</details>


### [54] [RetiWave-Mamba: A Dual-Stream Network for Retinal Disease Detection based on Multi-scale Context and Frequency-Adaptive Mamba Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17623)
*Cheng Cheng,Jin Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RetiWave-Mamba框架，结合空间-频率域学习与状态空间模型，通过DWT分解OCT图像为低频和高频流，分别用多尺度上下文定位模块和注意力引导高分辨率网络处理，并通过频率自适应Mamba投影器捕获长程依赖，在OCT-C8数据集上达到98.25%的分类准确率，取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视网膜疾病是导致不可逆视力损伤的主要原因，早期准确诊断至关重要。OCT成像虽为关键模态，但其自动分析面临散斑噪声、病灶尺度多变和类间细微相似三大挑战，现有方法难以同时有效应对这些问题。

Method: 提出RetiWave-Mamba框架：(1) 利用DWT将OCT图像分解为低频和高频流进行解耦处理；(2) 低频分支设计MCLM模块，通过多尺度扩张与空间注意力协同扩大全局感受野并精确定位病灶；(3) 高频分支引入AG-HRNet，配备智能门控机制抑制噪声在多尺度交互中的传播；(4) 集成FAMP投影器捕获非连续高频纹理特征中的长程依赖。

Result: 在OCT-C8数据集上的实验表明，该方法达到98.25%的分类准确率，超越现有方法，取得SOTA性能，验证了在噪声条件下稳健识别视网膜病变的有效性。

Conclusion: RetiWave-Mamba通过空间-频率域解耦学习与状态空间模型的结合，有效解决了OCT图像分析中的噪声干扰、尺度差异和类间相似性问题，为临床视网膜病变诊断提供了有前景的工具。

Abstract: Retinal diseases are a leading cause of irreversible vision impairment, making early and accurate diagnosis essential for effective treatment. Optical Coherence Tomography (OCT) serves as a critical imaging modality for this purpose, yet its automated analysis is hindered by inherent speckle noise, varying lesion scales, and subtle inter-class similarities. To address these challenges, we propose a novel framework, RetiWave-Mamba, which integrates spatial-frequency domain learning with state-of-the-art state space models. The framework utilizes Discrete Wavelet Transform (DWT) to decompose OCT images into low- and high-frequency streams, enabling decoupled processing of structural context and fine-grained details. For the low-frequency branch, we design a Multi-scale Contextual Localization Module (MCLM), which synergizes multi-scale dilation with spatial attention to expand the global receptive field and precisely localize lesion regions. For the high-frequency branch, we introduce an Attention-Guided High-Resolution Network (AG-HRNet) equipped with an intelligent gating mechanism to suppress noise propagation during multi-scale interactions. Furthermore, a Frequency-Adaptive Mamba Projector (FAMP) is incorporated to capture long-range dependencies within disjoint high-frequency textural features. Extensive experiments on the OCT-C8 dataset demonstrate that our approach achieves a state-of-the-art (SOTA) classification accuracy of 98.25%, surpassing existing methods. These results highlight the efficacy of RetiWave-Mamba in robustly identifying retinal pathologies under noisy conditions, offering a promising tool for clinical diagnosis.

</details>


### [55] [MaLViL: Multi-axis Low-rank Vision-LSTM for Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17635)
*Afshin Bozorgpour,Sina Ghorbani Kolahi,Moein Heidari,Ilker Hacihaliloglu,Dorit Merhof*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MaLViL是一种多轴低秩Vision-LSTM网络，通过将ViL扩展到解码器各分辨率层级，在保持细粒度解剖细节的同时，将ViL算子内存消耗最高降低83倍，并在皮肤病变、超声和多器官CT分割基准上达到竞争性或最先进的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Vision-LSTM (ViL)虽然实现了高效的全局建模，但其计算成本随空间token数量增长，导致现有分割器只能将ViL限制在粗瓶颈层，从而丢失细粒度解剖细节。此外，将2D特征栅格化为1D序列会破坏正交扫描轴上的邻接关系。

Method: 提出三个核心组件：(1) 双向低秩ViL (Bi-LRViL)，在紧凑正交子空间上进行推理，并通过正交残差保留细节；(2) 尺度感知SaLViL，在序列化之前恢复跨轴邻接关系；(3) 跨方向混合器 (CDM) 融合正交水平和垂直遍历路径。此外，统计引导的跳跃调制 (SGSM) 保留编码器跳跃连接中的边界线索。

Result: 在皮肤病变、超声和多器官CT分割基准上达到竞争性或最先进的精度，同时将ViL算子内存消耗降低多达83倍（在细粒度解码器分辨率下）。

Conclusion: MaLViL成功克服了ViL在细粒度分割中的内存瓶颈和空间邻接丢失问题，通过低秩化与多轴融合策略在计算效率与分割精度之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Vision-LSTM (ViL) enables efficient global modeling, but its cost still scales with the number of spatial tokens, so existing segmenters confine ViL to a coarse bottleneck and lose fine anatomical detail. Rasterizing 2D features into a 1D sequence further breaks adjacency across the orthogonal scan axis. We propose MaLViL, a Multi-axis Low-rank Vision-LSTM network that extends ViL across decoder resolutions. Bidirectional low-rank ViL (Bi-LRViL) reasons on a compact orthonormal subspace and preserves detail through an orthogonal residual; scale-aware SaLViL restores cross-axis neighbors before serialization; and a Cross-Directional Mixer (CDM) fuses orthogonal horizontal and vertical traversal paths. Statistics-Guided Skip Modulation (SGSM) further retains boundary cues in encoder skips. On skin-lesion, ultrasound, and multi-organ CT benchmarks, MaLViL achieves competitive or state-of-the-art segmentation accuracy, while reducing ViL operator memory by up to $83\times$ at fine decoder resolutions. Code is available at: https://github.com/xmindflow/malvil.

</details>


### [56] [Denoised Variance-Based Pruning with Optimal Brain Bias Compensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17657)
*Geon Tack Lee,Jaegul Choo,Kang Eun Jeon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DVBP + OB²C方法，结合随机矩阵理论去噪激活方差谱以进行稳健的神经元选择，并通过将均值偏移补偿纳入最优脑压缩目标，实现结构化剪枝后剩余权重的闭式最优更新。实验表明该方法在DeiT、Swin和ConvNeXt架构上达到训练-free剪枝的最先进性能，在50% MLP剪枝率下可保留超过90%的Top-1准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Transformers在取得最先进性能的同时存在巨大的计算开销，限制了边缘设备部署。现有的结构化剪枝方法要么精度下降严重，要么需要昂贵的重训练。虽然基于方差的剪枝（VBP）提供了一种有前景的方案，但其受限于有限样本激活协方差的统计噪声，且仅依赖偏置更新的方式无法完全补偿结构重建误差。

Method: 本文提出DVBP + OB²C方法：1) 利用随机矩阵理论对激活协方差谱进行去噪，实现稳健的神经元选择；2) 数学上证明将均值偏移补偿纳入最优脑压缩（OBC）目标后，层间Hessian矩阵可精确约简为激活协方差矩阵；3) 利用与选择过程相同的统计量，实现对剩余权重的最优闭式更新。

Result: 在DeiT、Swin和ConvNeXt架构上的大量实验表明，DVBP + OB²C达到训练-free剪枝的最先进性能。在50% MLP剪枝率下，Small和Base变体可保留超过90%的原始Top-1准确率，相比VBP在ConvNeXt-T上提升高达29.46%，在Swin-S上提升7.33%。

Conclusion: DVBP + OB²C通过去噪协方差估计和最优权重补偿，有效解决了VBP方法中统计噪声和偏置更新不足的局限，在无需重训练的情况下实现了更优的ViT结构化剪枝性能。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) achieve state-of-the-art performance but carry massive computational overhead that restricts edge deployment. Although structural pruning has emerged as a key strategy to reduce these costs, existing methods often suffer from severe accuracy degradation or require expensive retraining. Recently, Variance-Based Pruning (VBP) introduced a promising paradigm by selecting neurons based on activation variance; however, it remains limited by statistical noise in finite-sample activation covariance and reliance on bias-only updates that cannot fully account for structural reconstruction error. To address these limitations, we introduce Denoised Variance-Based Pruning with Optimal Brain Bias Compensation (DVBP + OB$^2$C). We leverage random matrix theory to filter noise from the activation covariance spectrum for robust neuron selection and mathematically prove that integrating mean-shift compensation into the Optimal Brain Compression objective reduces the layer-wise Hessian exactly to the activation covariance matrix. This enables an optimal, closed-form update of the remaining weights using the same statistics gathered for selection. Extensive experiments on DeiT, Swin, and ConvNeXt architectures demonstrate that DVBP + OB$^2$C achieves state-of-the-art training-free performance; at 50% MLP pruning, it retains over 90% of the original Top-1 accuracy on Small and Base variants, outperforming VBP by up to 29.46% (ConvNeXt-T) and 7.33% (Swin-S). The code is available at: https://github.com/geontackee/DVBP_OB2C.

</details>


### [57] [Is Haar Enough? Exploring Symlets and Coiflets for Wavelet Convolution Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17662)
*Md Rifat Ur Rahman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了小波卷积中滤波器长度与分解层数之间的权衡（F-vs.-L tradeoff），并系统验证了具有更强逼近性质的小波基（如Coiflets）可以在更浅的分解深度下达到与Haar基相当的精度，同时减少约32%的参数和33%的FLOPs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 之前的小波卷积工作固定使用Haar或Daubechies小波基及特定的分解深度，尚未系统探索不同小波基对效率-精度前沿的影响。作者旨在回答：选择不同的小波基能否突破现有的效率边界。

Method: 作者将问题形式化为滤波器长度（F）与分解层数（L）之间的权衡，并在受控架构和计算预算下，对Haar、Daubechies、Symlets和Coiflets四类小波基进行系统性对比研究。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、ImageNet-1K图像分类和Cityscapes语义分割任务上，Coiflet小波卷积可以在更浅的分解深度匹配Haar在更深层的精度，同时减少约32%的额外参数和33%的额外FLOPs。

Conclusion: 更长的滤波器虽然增加了单层变换成本，但可通过降低所需分解深度实现整体参数和计算量的净减少，为实践者构建基于小波的架构提供了具体可行的设计指导。

Abstract: Wavelet convolution layers have recently emerged as an efficient mechanism for enlarging receptive fields through multiresolution analysis, but prior work has fixed the wavelet basis to Haar or Daubechies at a chosen decomposition depth, leaving open whether a different basis can shift the underlying efficiency frontier. We identify and characterize a previously unexplored trade-off in this setting: bases with stronger approximation properties (longer filters) can reduce the decomposition depth required for competitive accuracy, yielding a net reduction in parameters and FLOPs despite higher perlevel transform cost. We formalize this as an F-vs.-L tradeoff (filter length vs. decomposition levels) and study it systematically across Haar, Daubechies, Symlets, and Coiflets under controlled architectures and budgets. On image classification (CIFAR-10, ImageNet-1K) and semantic segmentation (Cityscapes), Coiflet-based wavelet convolutions match Haar at deeper levels with approximately 32% fewer additional parameters and 33% fewer additional FLOPs, providing a concrete and actionable design choice for practitioners building wavelet-based architectures.

</details>


### [58] [Differentiable Voronoi Ray Tracing Beyond Rasterization Speeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17682)
*Bernardo Taveira,Carl Lindström,Joakim Johnander,Fredrik Kahl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VoroTracing，一种基于Voronoi光线追踪的高性能实时新视角合成方法，通过场景表示、优化与GPU执行的协同设计，在保持重建质量的同时达到623 FPS（RTX 5090），性能为最快先验光线追踪方法的3.2倍、3D高斯泼溅的2.8倍，并天然支持鱼眼、卷帘快门、运动模糊和景深等非针孔效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的实时新视角合成主要依赖基于投影的光栅化显式基元（如3D高斯泼溅），这类方法吞吐量高但需要专门扩展才能处理畸变、卷帘快门、景深等非针孔效果；而基于光线的渲染虽能自然表达这些效果，但通常被认为太慢，难以满足实时渲染的要求。作者希望通过分析可微分Voronoi光线追踪的吞吐量瓶颈并加以优化，证明光线追踪同样可以实现实时性能。

Method: 作者首先分析了可微分Voronoi光线追踪中影响吞吐量的三个主要因素：遍历长度、每单元计算量和内存局部性。在此基础上提出VoroTracing，从三个方面协同优化：(1) 使用紧凑的八面体外观纹理以减少内存流量；(2) 采用表面集中的不透明度以促进光线提前终止；(3) 采用固定预算的场景表示，无需剪枝或增密操作，并设计了面向连贯遍历的GPU实现。

Result: 在Mip-NeRF 360基准上，VoroTracing在RTX 5090上达到623 FPS，吞吐量是最快先验光线追踪方法的3.2倍、3D高斯泼溅的2.8倍，同时重建质量与这些方法相当。渲染器通过光线生成和采样支持鱼眼、卷帘快门、运动模糊和景深效果，无需专门光栅化。

Conclusion: 这项工作证明实时吞吐量与光线追踪的灵活性可以兼得，为实时新视角合成提供了一种兼顾性能与特效表达能力的新途径。作者已发布源代码。

Abstract: Real-time novel view synthesis is dominated by rasterized explicit primitives. These projection-based pipelines provide high throughput but require specialized extensions for non-pinhole effects such as distortion, rolling shutter, and depth of field. Ray-based rendering expresses these effects naturally but is generally assumed too slow for competitive real-time rendering. We analyze the factors governing throughput in differentiable Voronoi ray tracing and identify traversal length, per-cell work, and memory locality as principal determinants. Guided by this, we introduce VoroTracing, which co-designs the scene representation, optimization, and GPU execution to reduce these costs. Compact octahedral appearance textures reduce memory traffic, while surface-concentrated opacity promotes early termination. The fixed-budget representation is optimized without pruning or densification and rendered with a GPU implementation designed for coherent traversal. On Mip-NeRF 360, VoroTracing renders at 623 FPS on an RTX 5090, providing $3.2\times$ the throughput of the fastest prior ray-based method and $2.8\times$ that of 3D Gaussian Splatting, while maintaining competitive reconstruction quality. Our renderer supports fisheye, rolling-shutter, motion-blur, and depth-of-field effects through ray generation and sampling, requiring no specialized rasterization. These results show that real-time throughput can be achieved with the flexibility of ray-based rendering. We release our source code, see https://research.zenseact.com/publications/vorotracing

</details>


### [59] [Magnitude-Direction Decoupling for Fast Video Generation with Flow Matching Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17695)
*Haonan Xu,Feiyang Chen,Songkui Chen,Hongpeng Pan,Zhefeng Wang,Xinyu Duan,Baoxing Huai,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种面向视频生成Flow Matching模型的加速推理方法MDD，通过幅值-方向解耦策略，用轻量模型替代原模型进行去噪，结合缓存的方向引导，在保持视觉质量的同时实现最高2.95倍的推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flow matching视频生成模型虽性能优异，但迭代式去噪过程计算开销巨大。已有加速方法（如缓存或轻量模型替代）会偏离原始去噪轨迹，导致生成质量下降。因此需要一种既能加速又不损失保真度的可靠方案。

Method: 通过经验分析发现：轻量模型能稳健捕捉原模型输出的幅值（magnitude）分量，而缓存能提供可靠的方向（direction）引导。基于此提出Magnitude-Direction Decoupling（MDD）方法，自适应地使用方向校准的轻量模型替代原模型进行推理加速，并纠正去噪轨迹偏差。此外，在分类器无关引导（CFG）下通过重用幅值信息进一步降低推理成本。

Result: MDD在多个基准上优于现有加速方法，在Wan2.1上实现最高2.95倍的推理加速，同时保持高视觉保真度和内容丰富度。

Conclusion: MDD提供了一种可靠且轻量的视频生成加速方案，通过幅值与方向的解耦利用，在显著提升推理速度的同时保持生成质量，为Flow matching视频生成模型的高效部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: Flow matching models for video generation achieve impressive performance but suffer from high computational overhead due to iterative denoising. In fact, the original model is not necessary for all denoising steps, allowing some steps to use lightweight alternatives for faster sampling. However, directly using caching or lightweight models can deviate from the original denoising trajectory, resulting in suboptimal performance. Through empirical analysis, we find that lightweight models can robustly capture the magnitude components of the original model's output, while caching provides reliable directional guidance. Building on this insight, we propose the Magnitude-Direction Decoupling (MDD) method, which adaptively employs a direction-calibrated lightweight model as a substitute for the original model to accelerate inference and effectively correct deviations in the denoising trajectory. Moreover, MDD further reduces inference costs by reusing magnitude information under classifier-free guidance (CFG). As a result, MDD offers a more reliable and lightweight solution to accelerate sampling. Experiments show that MDD outperforms existing acceleration methods, delivering promising speedups (e.g., up to 2.95x on Wan2.1) while preserving high visual fidelity and content richness.

</details>


### [60] [Environment-Invariant Subspace Learning for Generalizable Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17700)
*Shenghao Chen,Hao Jia,Chen Li,Chunjie Ma,Zan Gao,Shengyong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出环境不变子空间学习（EISL）框架，通过可学习的低秩投影将特征解耦为正交的伪造相关不变因子与环境相关残差因子，并结合环境干预模块生成多样化的干预对，以增强深伪检测在跨分布场景下的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深伪检测中的跨分布泛化仍是关键瓶颈。虽然近期工作利用大规模视觉基础模型（VFM）的语义先验，但这些语义先验容易受到光照、风格等环境因素的干扰，导致伪造线索与环境模式之间建立虚假相关性，严重限制了模型的泛化能力。本文旨在解决这一根本性挑战。

Method: 提出EISL框架，核心通过一个可学习的低秩投影将特征解耦为正交的伪造相关不变因子和环境相关残差因子；同时设计环境干预（Environmental Intervention）模块，生成多样化且具有挑战性的干预对，模拟分布外的环境变化，引导模型发现真正不变的伪造表征。

Result: 在跨数据集、跨生成器、全脸合成和腐败（corruption）设置下的实验均显示一致的性能提升，相比强检测器达到具有竞争力或领先的表现，展示了对未见伪造类型和环境变化的改进鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文为理解和解决VFM在深伪检测中泛化障碍提供了新视角和有价值的探索，验证了通过环境不变子空间学习来增强深伪检测跨分布泛化能力的有效性。

Abstract: Cross-distribution generalization remains a critical bottleneck in deepfake detection. While recent efforts leverage the semantic priors of large-scale visual foundation models (VFMs), a noteworthy yet underexplored challenge remains: the susceptibility of these semantic priors to environmental interference from factors such as lighting and style. Crucially, this interference establishes spurious correlations between forgery cues and environmental patterns that severely limit generalization. To address this fundamental challenge, we propose an innovative Environment-Invariant Subspace Learning (EISL) framework. The core contribution of EISL is that it aims to disentangle features into orthogonal forgery-relevant invariant factors and environment-related residual factors via a learnable low-rank projection. To facilitate robust feature disentanglement, we also design an Environmental Intervention module that generates diverse and challenging intervention pairs, simulating out-of-distribution environmental shifts to guide the model toward discovering truly invariant forgery representations. Experiments across cross-dataset, cross-generator, whole-face synthesis, and corruption settings show consistent gains and competitive or leading performance against strong detectors, demonstrating improved robustness to unseen forgery types and environmental variations. This work provides a new perspective and a valuable exploration for understanding and tackling the generalization barriers of VFMs in deepfake detection.

</details>


### [61] [Monitoring Pasture Restoration from Satellite Image Time Series: Caveats and Opportunities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17704)
*Linnea Sartorius,Isak Randahl,Delia Fano Yela,Georg Andersson,Sadegh Jamali,Aleksis Pirinen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次将牧场恢复状态检测建模为二分类深度学习问题，利用Sentinel-2卫星图像时间序列，在1,397个瑞典恢复牧场数据上实现0.88准确率，并揭示时间相关混淆因素对可靠部署的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模监测自然恢复是一个重要但困难的生态问题。半自然草原的恢复效果是渐进发展的，但卫星观测受天气、采集条件和处理伪影影响，难以区分真实的恢复信号与无关的时间变异，亟需可靠的大规模监测方法。

Method: 将牧场恢复状态检测公式化为二分类深度学习问题，评估两种常见的SITS深度学习架构，采用不同的Sentinel-2图像组合，并对实验数据进行每牧场归一化处理以显式建模年内变异性。

Result: 在1,397个已恢复瑞典牧场上进行实验，最佳模型达到0.88准确率。偏倚分析发现，可靠部署需要时间上平衡的标签以及显式测试年份相关混淆的评估协议。

Conclusion: 本文贡献并非一个已解决的恢复监测系统，而是一个现实的案例研究，展示了哪些方法有效、哪些失败，以及未来研究应控制哪些变量。代码和模型已公开。

Abstract: Monitoring nature restoration at scale is an important but difficult ecological problem. Deep learning methods to analyze satellite image time series (SITS) have been widely used for land surface monitoring. In semi-natural grasslands - the habitat type in focus in this work - restoration outcomes develop gradually, yet satellite observations are influenced by weather, acquisition conditions, and processing artefacts, making it difficult to distinguish genuine restoration signals from unrelated temporal variation. In this work, we examine - to the best of our knowledge, for the first time - whether restoration status can be detected directly from satellite image time series by formulating pasture restoration as a binary deep learning classification problem. We evaluate two common SITS deep learning architectures on different Sentinel-2 image combinations, across 1,397 restored Swedish pastures and find that explicitly modeling intra-year variability and per-pasture normalization increases separability, reaching 0.88 accuracy for the best model. We further investigate our results and perform a targeted bias analysis finding that reliable deployment requires temporally balanced labels and evaluation protocols that explicitly test for year-related confounding. We therefore frame our contribution not as a solved restoration-monitoring system, but as a realistic case study of what works, what fails, and what future studies should control for. Code and models are available at https://github.com/aleksispi/ml-nature-resto.

</details>


### [62] [DynaForcing: Overcoming Dynamic Collapse in Self-Forcing Distillation for Streaming Avatar Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17707)
*Yubo Huang,Sirui Zhao,Xinchen Yao,Zhengye Zhang,Jinyang Huang,Fengqi Cui,Shiwei Wu,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DynaForcing框架，解决音频驱动虚拟人生成中自强制（self-forcing）与DMD蒸馏导致的动态坍塌（dynamic collapse）问题，通过混合强制、动力学感知奖励正则化、参考扰动三种策略恢复运动动态，同时将GPU开销降低一个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频驱动的虚拟人生成需要同时满足唇形同步、表情生动和实时流式生成。现有方法使用自强制配合分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）实现实时性，但存在一个尚未被系统表征的关键缺陷——动态坍塌：学生模型收敛到近静态的最优解，虽具高感知质量但时间动态被严重抑制，导致唇形同步和表情受损。这一缺陷源于DMD中反向KL目标对低运动模式的偏好，以及未锚定的自条件化形成的放大坍塌的反馈回路。

Method: 提出DynaForcing训练框架，包含三个不同层面的互补策略：（1）混合强制（Hybrid Forcing）在数据层面将rollout锚定到真实动态以打破反馈回路；（2）动力学感知奖励正则化（Dynamics-Aware Reward Regularization）通过DMD的强化学习解释引入显式运动奖励以抵消反向KL偏差；（3）参考扰动（Reference Perturbation）扰动参考图像以解耦身份与静态细节，迫使模型依赖音频驱动运动。此外引入计算图剪枝和梯度回放，将自强制的GPU开销降低一个数量级以上。

Result: 实验表明，DynaForcing将动态退化指标（Dyn-Deg）从0.31提升至0.73，同步置信度（Sync-C）从7.03提升至7.68，恢复至教师模型的动态水平，同时改善视觉质量，在整个训练过程中消解了质量-动态之间的权衡，无需早停。

Conclusion: DynaForcing有效解决了基于自强制DMD的音频驱动虚拟人生成中的动态坍塌问题，通过多层次策略协同恢复时间动态，在保持高视觉质量的同时实现接近教师模型的动力学性能，并大幅降低计算开销。该工作首次系统表征了自强制范式中的动态坍塌问题并提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Audio-driven avatar generation requires realistic lip-sync, expressive motion, and real-time streaming. Recent work achieves the latter via self-forcing with Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD), but this paradigm suffers from a critical failure that has not been systematically characterized: dynamic collapse, where the student model converges to a near-static optimum with high perceptual quality but severely suppressed temporal dynamics. We trace this to two causes: the reverse KL objective in DMD, which biases toward low-motion modes, and unanchored self-conditioning, which creates a feedback loop that amplifies collapse. This is especially harmful for avatars, where even subtle motion loss breaks lip-sync and expression.
  To address this, we propose DynaForcing, a training framework with three complementary strategies applied at different levels. Specifically, Hybrid Forcing anchors rollouts to ground-truth dynamics at the data level to break the feedback loop. Dynamics-Aware Reward Regularization introduces explicit motion rewards via the RL interpretation of DMD to counteract the reverse KL bias at the loss level. Reference Perturbation perturbs reference images to decouple identity from static details, forcing the model to rely on audio for motion at the conditioning level. We further introduce computation graph pruning and gradient replay, reducing the GPU footprint of self-forcing by over an order of magnitude. Experiments show that DynaForcing recovers dynamics to teacher-comparable levels (Dyn-Deg: 0.31 -> 0.73, Sync-C: 7.03 -> 7.68) while improving visual quality, resolving the quality-dynamics trade-off throughout training without early stopping.

</details>


### [63] [Vision-Language Models for Analog Gauge Reading: An Empirical Study of Specialization, Transfer and Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17723)
*Abdul Mueez,Aaditya Baranwal,Junior Chaj-Mejia,Guneet Bhatia,Jason T. Voelker,Shruti Vyas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了通用视觉语言模型（Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct）在模拟仪表直接数值读取任务上的专化、迁移、鲁棒性和可靠性，通过零样本、上下文学习和QLoRA参数高效微调三种方式在合成、视频衍生及工业专有数据集上测试，最佳微调平均百分比误差（MPE）为2.39%（合成）、2.61%（压力表）和4.43%（工业数据），但跨数据集迁移性能显著退化，高斯模糊是最强干扰，且高置信度错误仍可能发生，因此支持QLoRA专化VLM用于单表读取，但尚不足以构成部署就绪的工厂监测流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟仪表在工业环境中仍大量存在，人工抄表成本高且存在安全隐患。现有方法多依赖显式的指针分割和几何读数管线，通用视觉语言模型能否在不借助此类管线的情况下直接实现数值读取，尚缺乏系统性的专化、迁移、鲁棒性和可靠性评估。

Method: 采用Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct模型，评估三种策略：零样本提示、上下文学习（ICL）和基于量化低秩适配（QLoRA）的参数高效微调。实验在三个数据集上进行：公开合成数据集、从视频衍生的压力表数据集和专有工业数据集。所有微调实验采用固定的20-epoch协议并使用最终epoch进行分析；分别训练带/不带表盘量程信息的模型以消除提示设置混淆。主要评估指标为量程归一化后的平均百分比误差（MPE），并使用95%自助法置信区间。此外执行留一数据集交叉实验评估迁移性能，对多种图像扰动进行鲁棒性测试，并分析模型置信度与错误率之间的可靠性关系。

Result: 最佳微调MPE在合成数据集上为2.39%（CI: 1.43-3.90%），压力表数据集为2.61%（CI: 1.66-3.80%），专有工业数据集为4.43%（CI: 2.31-7.14%）。留一数据集实验显示在保留的合成和专有数据上存在显著的迁移退化。鲁棒性测试发现高斯模糊是最强的测试扰动。可靠性分析表明高置信度错误仍然可能存在。

Conclusion: QLoRA微调能够使通用VLM在单目标模拟仪表直接读数上达到可接受的精度水平（MPE低于5%），但跨数据集泛化能力有限，鲁棒性受高斯模糊等退化影响显著，且可靠性不足以排除高置信度错误。因此，该方法支持作为单表读取的工具，但在安全关键的工厂监测场景中尚不能作为部署就绪的完整流水线，需要引入弃权机制和独立验证。

Abstract: Analog gauges remain common in industrial environments where manual inspection is costly or hazardous. The engineering application addressed here is direct numerical reading of single-target analog-gauge images, while the artificial-intelligence contribution is a systematic evaluation of specialization, transfer, robustness and reliability for a general-purpose vision-language model (VLM) without an explicit pointer-segmentation and geometric-reading pipeline. The Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct model is evaluated using zero-shot prompting, in-context learning (ICL) and parameter-efficient fine-tuning with Quantized Low-Rank Adaptation (QLoRA) on a public synthetic dataset, a video-derived Pressure Gauge dataset and a proprietary industrial dataset. All fine-tuning experiments use a fixed 20-epoch protocol with the final epoch used for analysis; separate models with and without supplied gauge ranges remove prompt-setting confounds. The primary metric is range-normalized mean percentage error (MPE). The best fine-tuned MPE values are 2.39% on the synthetic dataset, with a 95% bootstrap confidence interval (CI) of 1.43-3.90%; 2.61% on the Pressure Gauge dataset, with a CI of 1.66-3.80%; and 4.43% on the proprietary industrial dataset, with a CI of 2.31-7.14%. Leave-one-dataset-out experiments reveal substantial transfer degradation on held-out synthetic and proprietary data, while robustness tests identify Gaussian blur as the strongest tested corruption. Reliability analysis shows that high-confidence errors remain possible, motivating abstention and independent validation in safety-critical use. These results support QLoRA-specialized VLMs for direct single-gauge reading but not yet a deployment-ready plant-monitoring pipeline.

</details>


### [64] [Evaluation of AI-based Visual Crack Detection in Steel Bridges Using Probability of Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17726)
*Andrii Kompanets,Finn Michael Sherry,Remco Duits,Davide Leonetti,H. H. Snijder*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于概率检测曲线（POD）的统计评估框架，用于将计算机视觉裂缝检测方法与传统视觉检查方法进行工程兼容性比较，并在真实钢桥裂缝数据集上验证了该方法的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机视觉损伤检测方法通常使用IoU、mAP等指标进行评估，但这些指标难以预测方法在实际结构工程中的有效性，阻碍了这些方法在工程实践中的系统化应用。因此需要一种与标准工程方法兼容的性能评估方式。

Method: 提出一个基于概率检测曲线（probability of detection curves）的新型统计评估框架，该框架能够考虑图像分辨率的影响，并将计算机视觉方法的检测性能与传统视觉检查方法进行对比。应用于真实的"Cracks in Steel Bridges"数据集进行验证。

Result: 通过概率检测及其不确定性的量化，实现了对自动化损伤检测方法在结构可靠性分析中实际效果的实践评估，证明了所提出的计算机视觉方法在裂缝检测任务上是稳健的，并且作为传统视觉检查方法的补充具有高附加价值。

Conclusion: 该评估框架填补了计算机视觉指标与工程实践需求之间的鸿沟，为基于AI的损伤检测方法在安全关键应用中的广泛使用铺平了道路。

Abstract: Bridge structures are regularly inspected for structural damage such as cracks and corrosion in order to ensure public safety and reduce maintenance costs. Much research has been done on automating this process using computer vision methods, which are often evaluated and compared using metrics such as intersection over union, mean average precision, etc. However, predicting the actual effectiveness of an inspection method within the field of structural engineering from these metrics remains challenging. To enable the systematic use of these increasingly popular methods in engineering practice, evaluating the performance of these methods in a way that is compatible with standard engineering approaches is therefore an urgent necessity. We present a new statistical evaluation framework to allow the comparison of computer vision methods with conventional visual inspection for crack detection in steel bridges. The framework is based on probability of detection curves and can account for the influence of image resolution. We apply this evaluation method to the real-world ``Cracks in Steel Bridges'' dataset, which contains annotated images of cracks in bridge structures. The quantification of the probability of detection and its uncertainty enables a practical assessment of the effect of automated methods for damage detection in structural reliability analyses. In turn, this enables the wide-spread use of automated (AI-based) damage detection in safety critical applications. This evaluation method provides evidence that the proposed computer vision approach approach is robust for the crack detection task and can have a high added value as an addition to conventional visual inspection methods.

</details>


### [65] [TINA+: Probing Residual Visual Knowledge in Unlearned Diffusion Models via Diffusion-Consistent Text-Free Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17747)
*Qianlong Xiang,Miao Zhang,Kun Wang,Haoyu Zhang,Junhui Hou,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TINA+，一种基于扩散一致性的无文本反转攻击方法，用于从视觉角度探测概念擦除后是否仍残留视觉知识，实验表明现有擦除方法大多只是切断文本-图像映射而非真正消除底层视觉知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的概念擦除和对抗性探测方法主要从文本视角出发，只关注文本到图像的映射是否被切断，却忽略了擦除概念对应的视觉知识是否仍然存在于模型中。作者希望从视觉角度探究这一问题，即通过扩散反转来验证生成轨迹是否能重建已擦除概念的视觉实例。

Method: TINA+包含两个核心组件：（1）优化式反转（optimization-based inversion），在空文本条件下避免文本通路但修正放大近似误差；（2）扩散一致性轨迹正则化（Diffusion-Consistent Trajectory Regularization），通过惩罚远低于扩散预期边际能量演化的轨迹来抑制虚假反转路径，防止随机初始化的扩散模型被错误判定为保留了目标概念。

Result: 在涵盖十二种擦除方法、四个概念擦除任务和不同模型架构的实验上，TINA+能够可靠地通过扩散一致的视觉轨迹探测残留视觉知识。实验结果提供了更强证据，表明当前方法往往通过切断文本-图像链接来掩盖概念，而非真正消除底层视觉知识。

Conclusion: TINA+揭示了现有概念擦除方法的局限性：它们虽然在文本-图像映射层面实现了擦除，但底层视觉知识仍然存在，可通过扩散一致的视觉轨迹被探测到。这为概念擦除技术的改进方向提供了新的视觉维度洞察。
